Result: MINT在分布内和分布外预测问答任务上均达到最先进的性能，与文本序列化基线相比，大幅降低了输入令牌数量、延迟和内存消耗。全面的分析表明紧凑的交易嵌入优于文本序列化方法。

Conclusion: 紧凑的交易嵌入是一种比文本序列化更优越的交易表示方法，适用于多模态推理和零样本预测任务，为支付领域的高效零样本推理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Banks analyse sequential financial transaction data to perform many tasks, including fraud prevention, credit risk assessment and offer personalization. To improve the predictive accuracy of these tasks, Payments Foundation Models encode transaction sequence data as rich contextual embeddings, which can then be provided to task-specific models as features. However, these Foundation Models are not designed for flexible zero-shot reasoning across novel downstream prediction tasks, limiting their adaptability and utility. Existing LLM-based approaches to zero-shot prediction often fail to fully exploit the predictive signal within transaction data, while relying on costly text serialization or task-specific architectures that scale poorly. To address these limitations, we present the Multimodal Instruction Network for Transactions (MINT), a framework that connects a pretrained transaction sequence encoder to a decoder-only LLM through lightweight embedding injection, transaction-language alignment, and instruction tuning. We find that MINT achieves state-of-the-art predictive question-answering performance in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution questions, while substantially reducing input tokens, latency, and memory consumption compared to text-serialization baselines. Through comprehensive analyses of representations, alignment strategies, training data, and history length, we establish that compact transaction embeddings are a superior approach to transaction representation than text serialization for multimodal reasoning and zero-shot prediction tasks.

</details>


### [138] [Adaptive Protection for Evolutionary Feature Construction in Symbolic Regression with Application to Credit Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14209)
*Hengzhe Zhang,Qi Chen,Bing Xue,Lean Yu,Wolfgang Banzhaf,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性指标在进化过程中选择性保留已构造的特征，防止有价值的遗传材料丢失，从而提升符号回归及其他搜索任务的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化特征构造在符号回归中表现出潜力，但现有方法缺乏显式机制来保留进化过程中发现的重要构造特征，遗传算子可能破坏有效特征导致有价值的遗传材料丢失。

Method: 提出自适应保护机制，基于特征重要性指标对构造特征进行选择性保护：更重要的特征获得更强保护，次要特征仍可被修改并可整合来自重要特征的有用构建模块。

Result: 在98个回归基准数据集上，该机制相比基线方法一致地提升了解质量；在两个信用分类数据集上也验证了方法可有效扩展到符号回归以外的搜索任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应特征保护机制是有效且鲁棒的，能够跨多种特征重要性计算方法与不同基础学习器保持一致性能提升，并具有超越符号回归的通用适用性。

Abstract: Evolutionary feature construction has shown strong promise in symbolic regression by automatically discovering informative transformations of input features that enhance a simple base learner. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to preserve important constructed features discovered during evolution, and valuable genetic material can be lost when genetic operators disrupt effective features. This paper introduces an adaptive protection mechanism that leverages feature importance metrics to selectively preserve constructed features during evolution. The mechanism provides stronger protection for more important constructed features while still allowing less important features to be modified and to incorporate useful building blocks from more important features. We evaluate the approach using multiple feature importance calculation methods and demonstrate its robustness across different base learners. Experimental results on 98 regression benchmark datasets show that the proposed mechanism consistently improves solution quality over baseline approaches, and experiments on two credit classification datasets demonstrate that the method also extends effectively to improve search effectiveness beyond symbolic regression.

</details>


### [139] [Training Fair Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14211)
*Patrik Kenfack,Jesse C. Cresswell,Anthony L. Caterini,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairTFM，一种将公平性约束直接融入表格基础模型（TFM）训练的策略，通过合成公平性任务和基于梯度反转层的公平感知架构，使模型能够在单次前向传播中实现公平预测，在132个公平性任务上验证了其公平性与准确性的平衡效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在高风险决策中的应用日益广泛，但其公平性属性尚未得到充分探索。现有公平性技术无法与上下文学习（in-context learning）范式兼容，且训练数据中敏感属性的访问受限，这两大挑战亟需解决。

Method: FairTFM的核心包含两部分：(1) 基于合成公平性任务的可扩展训练策略，解决敏感属性数据稀缺问题；(2) 采用梯度反转层（Gradient Reversal Layer）的公平感知架构，促使模型学习对敏感属性不变的表示。

Result: 在132个公平性任务上的实验表明，FairTFM在保持具有竞争力的准确率的同时，实现了公平性的一致改善。

Conclusion: FairTFM成功地将公平性约束引入TFM训练，实现了无需任务特定训练即可在单次前向传播中做出公平预测，为表格基础模型在高风险场景中的公平部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as leading methods for tabular predictive tasks, leveraging in-context learning to predict on new data without task-specific training. Despite the increased use of TFMs in high-stakes decision-making, their fairness properties remain largely unexplored. In this work, we incorporate fairness constraints directly into TFM training, enabling fair predictions in a single forward pass. Our approach addresses two key challenges: limited access to sensitive attributes in training data, and the incompatibility of existing fairness techniques with the in-context learning paradigm. We propose FairTFM, a scalable training strategy based on synthetic fairness tasks and a fairness-aware architecture using a gradient reversal layer, which encourages the model to learn representations invariant to sensitive attributes. Experiments on 132 fairness tasks show consistent improvements in fairness while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [140] [Connected Subspace Clustering: Hardness, a Scalable Heuristic, and an Application to Sea Level Geodesy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14215)
*Johanna Hillebrand,Jan Höckendorff,Jürgen Kusche,Kelin Luo,Heiko Röglin,Melanie Schmidt,Christian Sohler,Bernd Uebbing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连通子空间聚类问题（Connected Subspace Clustering），用于在高维空间中将彼此相似且物理连贯的数据点分组为k个连通簇。论文证明即使在m'=0和带洞网格图上该问题也难以近似，并提出一种Lloyd风格启发式算法，交替进行子空间拟合和迭代合并来保证连通性。在地球物理应用中，该方法比无约束方法少产生多达1,966个不连通碎片，并在160个配置的实验中73.75%的情况下优于四种修复策略中的其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典聚类方法仅关注簇内相似性，忽略了测量变量在不同物理位置时的空间连贯性需求。受大地测量学中需识别海水表面连续区域进行主成分分析的场景启发，作者引入了连通子空间聚类问题，即在高维数据中同时保证簇内部相似（最佳拟合仿射子空间）和簇间物理连通（图约束），以解决实际科学应用中的约束聚类需求。

Method: 首先形式化定义连通子空间聚类问题：给定高维点和连通图，将数据划分为k个连通簇，最小化各簇到最佳m'维仿射子空间的平方距离总和。其次证明问题的近似难度下界。然后提出Lloyd风格的启发式算法，交替执行子空间拟合和迭代合并过程以强制执行连通性约束，确保最终恰好产生k个连通区域。

Result: 理论上证明即使在最简单情形（m'=0、带洞网格图）下，问题也难以在Ω(n^{1/2-ε})近似因子内求解。实验方面，在160个全球海平面时间序列配置中，基于合并的修复策略在73.75%的情况下优于其他三种策略，且一致优于（连通）Ward方法在所有测试簇数下的表现。无约束方法会留下多达1,966个不连通碎片，而作者方法保证恰好k个连通区域。发现的区域与厄尔尼诺-南方涛动和印度洋偶极子等气候指数信号吻合。

Conclusion: 作者提出的连通子空间聚类方法有效解决了有空间连贯性约束的高维聚类问题，不仅在地球物理学中取得了优于现有方法的结果，而且方法具有通用性，可推广到气候场、遥感、神经影像和传感器网络等其他空间嵌入的多元时间序列分析场景。

Abstract: Constrained optimization extends classical optimization by integrating side information, making it widely applicable across scientific and engineering domains. Consider a setting where we measure variables at different physical locations. When grouping these measurements, we often want clusters that are both internally similar and physically coherent. Thus, we have a constrained clustering problem where the constraint models coherence. Motivated by an application in geodesy, where contiguous regions of the sea surface must be identified for principal component analysis, we introduce the Connected Subspace Clustering problem: given high-dimensional points and a connectivity graph, partition them into $k$ connected clusters, minimizing their total squared distance to the clusters' best-fit $m'$-dimensional affine subspaces. We prove that, even for $m' = 0$ and a grid graph with holes, the problem is NP-hard to approximate within $Ω(n^{1/2-\varepsilon})$ for every $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ is the number of measurements. We then introduce an efficient Lloyd-style heuristic that alternates subspace fitting with an iterative merging procedure to enforce connectivity. Our method returns exactly $k$ connected regions by construction, whereas unconstrained methods leave up to $1{,}966$ disconnected fragments at higher cost. In a study of 160 configurations on global sea level time series, our merging-based repair is the strongest of four strategies in $73.75\%$ of cases, and consistently outperforms competitors such as (connected) Ward's method across all tested cluster counts. The resulting regions isolate signals aligning with climate indices such as the El Nino-Southern Oscillation and Indian Ocean Dipole. Although developed for geodesy, the approach applies to other spatially embedded multivariate time series, such as climate fields, remote sensing, neuroimaging, and sensor networks.

</details>


### [141] [AutoSchema: Live Schema Grounding for Agentic Text-to-Sparql over Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14228)
*Yiming Zhang,Koji Tsuda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoSchema框架，通过实时模式接地（live schema grounding）让语言模型代理直接从当前SPARQL端点获取模式证据，无需训练即可构建KGQA查询，在生物医学KGQA、纵向BioASQ基准及化学知识图谱迁移任务上优于TogoMCP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生命科学知识图谱各资源使用不同的schema、标识符和链接，TogoMCP虽然通过MIME文件帮助语言模型代理查询，但创建和维护这些文件仍需要人工辅助，成本高昂且难以扩展到不规则或未见过的图。

Method: AutoSchema是一个无需训练的通用框架，核心流程包括：检查实时schema、将问题中的实体名称映射到图标识符、探索关系路径，并在迭代查询构建过程中发现资源间的可能连接。

Result: 在生物医学KGQA任务中平均事实性准确率优于TogoMCP，在纵向BioASQ评估中取得一致提升；减少了迭代预算耗尽情况，核心评估中平均工具调用次数更少；化学知识图谱迁移研究表明实时模式接地可支持不规则和未见过的图。

Conclusion: 实时模式接地作为一种无需预先创建规范化schema文件的方案，能够有效提升语言模型代理在多种知识图谱上的查询能力，尤其在资源多样性和可扩展性方面具有优势。

Abstract: Life science knowledge graphs make large collections of structured data available through SPARQL, but each resource uses its own schema, identifiers, and links. TogoMCP helps language model agents query these resources by providing curated Metadata Interoperability Exchange files. Creating and maintaining these files still requires language model assisted drafting, validation, and manual review. We study \emph{live schema grounding}, where an agent obtains the schema evidence needed for a question directly from the current endpoints. We present \textsc{autoschema}, a general framework for live schema grounding that requires no training. It inspects live schemas, maps entity names in a question to graph identifiers, explores relation paths, and finds possible connections between resources during iterative query construction. We use TogoMCP as our main comparison framework. We evaluate \textsc{autoschema} on Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA, Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA, Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA over BioASQ Task B, and Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer to a previously undocumented RDF graph. \textsc{autoschema} improves mean factoid accuracy over TogoMCP in the biomedical KGQA tasks and gives consistent gains in the longitudinal BioASQ evaluation. It also reduces iteration budget exhaustion and uses fewer tool calls on average in the core evaluation. The transfer study gives preliminary evidence that live schema grounding can support irregular and previously unseen graphs without first creating a curated schema file.

</details>


### [142] [Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14264)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Vamshi Bonagiri,Raul Astudillo,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOBO-Merge，一种基于多目标贝叶斯优化的模型合并参数选择框架，在有限评估预算下逼近Pareto前沿，实验表明其在多数设置下优于随机搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并虽节省算力，但合并参数的选取困难：下游评估成本高、梯度不可用、源能力可能存在冲突，亟需高效的参数搜索方法。

Method: 将合并参数选择建模为黑盒多目标优化问题，采用多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）框架，与具体合并算子无关，可在有限评估预算下近似Pareto前沿。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上，使用Linear、SLERP、TIES及块级合并算子测试，MOBO-Merge在12组比较中有11组获得更高平均超体积；其中TIES在四组中三组表现最佳，Block-Linear 4x在Llama三模型合并中最强。

Conclusion: 多目标贝叶斯优化作为表达性合并参数化的搜索层具有显著价值，但不存在统一最优的合并算子，需根据具体的模型族和设置选择。

Abstract: Model merging combines trained models directly in weight space, offering a compute-efficient alternative to additional fine-tuning. Selecting merge parameters is nevertheless difficult because downstream evaluations are expensive, gradients are unavailable, and source capabilities can conflict. We formulate merge-parameter selection as a black-box multi-objective optimization problem and introduce MOBO-Merge, a merge-operator agnostic framework that uses multi-objective Bayesian optimization to approximate the Pareto front under a limited evaluation budget. We evaluate Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B in two-model instruction-math and three-model instruction-math-code settings using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and block-wise merge operators. On held-out benchmark partitions, MOBO-Merge obtains higher mean hypervolume than random search in 11 of 12 reported comparisons. The gain is small for one-dimensional Linear interpolation but substantially larger for several TIES, block-wise, and three-objective searches. No merge operator is uniformly best: TIES leads in three of four family-setting combinations, whereas Block-Linear 4x is strongest for the Llama three-model merge. These results show that multi-objective Bayesian optimization is valuable as a search layer for expressive merge parameterizations.

</details>


### [143] [Convex losses and their applications to SVM, SVR, and Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288)
*Filippo Portera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多种用于SVM和神经网络二分类任务的新型凸损失函数，通过粒子群优化算法求解原始SVM问题，证明新损失是标准损失的推广，并通过嵌套交叉验证评估性能，结果显示新损失与标准损失在泛化性能上无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在损失函数中利用模式相关性（pattern correlations）来提升分类模型的泛化性能，作为一种初步的理论与实践探索。

Method: 提出多种新型凸损失函数；由于对偶SVM模型存在实际局限性，采用SVM原始形式和神经网络配合新损失；使用粒子群优化算法求解原始SVM问题；通过嵌套交叉验证评估各损失的性能表现。

Result: 新损失是标准损失的推广；在多个小数据集上的初步实验表明，在损失函数中利用模式相关性理论上可能增强某些数据集的泛化性能，但实际结果表明泛化度量在有/无新损失的情况下相同。

Conclusion: 尽管新损失在理论上具有推广意义，但当前实验结果未能证明其对泛化性能有实质性提升，该研究属于初步探索性工作。

Abstract: We propose multiple new convex losses for SVM and Neural Networks, applied to binary classification tasks. While there are practical limitations in exploiting them with the dual SVM models, we are able to use them with SVM primal formulation and Neural Networks. In detail, the primal SVM problem with the modified losses has been solved with the Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm. We prove that the proposed losses are a generalization of the standard loss, and we experiment them with several small data-sets. This preliminary study shows that using pattern correlations
  inside the loss function could in theory enhance the generalization performances on some data-sets. To evaluate the performance of each loss, we adopt a Nested Cross-Validation procedure. Results show that generalization measures are the same with or without the new losses.

</details>


### [144] [Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303)
*Francesco Quinzan,Noor Munir,Yishun Lu,Stephen Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeSIFT，一种不依赖预定义威胁模型的代码生成提示检测方法，通过影响函数衡量生成代码对模型参数空间的影响，并利用统计检验判断候选提示是否偏离良性参考分布。在两个基准数据集、三个开源代码LLM（3B-7B参数）上评估，AUROC最高达0.98，显著优于静态分析基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM代码生成的防御方法通常依赖预定义威胁模型或已知漏洞模式，面对新型攻击时效果有限，缺乏威胁模型无关的通用检测方法。

Method: CodeSIFT采用影响函数（influence functions）来衡量生成代码在模型参数空间中的影响，并利用统计检验判断候选提示集是否偏离良性参考分布，从而识别诱导异常行为的恶意提示。

Result: 在三个3B-7B参数的开源代码LLM上，AUROC最高达0.98（中高注入率下），并保持校准良好的假阳性率，显著优于静态分析基线。此外还构建了两个覆盖多种漏洞类型的基准数据集。

Conclusion: 影响函数驱动的检测方法在识别恶意代码生成提示方面具有前景，无需预先了解攻击类别即可有效工作，为LLM代码生成安全提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet they remain vulnerable to prompts that elicit insecure implementations. Existing defenses typically rely on predefined threat models or known vulnerability patterns, limiting their effectiveness against novel attacks. We propose CodeSIFT, a threat-model-agnostic detection method that leverages influence functions to identify batches of prompts that induce anomalous model behavior. Rather than detecting specific vulnerabilities, CodeSIFT measures the parameter-space influence of generated code and uses a statistical test to determine whether a candidate prompt set deviates from a benign reference distribution. To evaluate our approach, we introduce two benchmark datasets covering a variety of vulnerabilities. We evaluate CodeSIFT on three open-weight code LLMs ranging from 3B to 7B parameters, achieving AUROC scores of up to 0.98 at moderate-to-high injection rates, while maintaining well-calibrated false positive rates and substantially outperforming static analysis baselines. These results suggest that influence-function-based detection is a promising direction for identifying malicious code-generation prompts without requiring prior knowledge of the underlying attack class.

</details>


### [145] [Quantum Multi-Armed Bandits and Linear Bandits: Lower Bounds and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14319)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子多臂老虎机（QMAB）和量子线性老虎机（QLB）的遗憾下界与算法设计，首次证明了QMAB的Ω(K log(T/K))和有限动作QLB的Ω(d log(T/d))极小极大下界，并提出了一个基于设计消除的算法，将有限动作QLB的遗憾从d²改进到d（匹配下界至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作分别给出了QMAB的O(K log T)和d维QLB的O(d² polylog T)遗憾上界，但K log T量级是否不可避免以及d²依赖能否改进这两个核心问题仍未解决。Wan等人[2023]提出的"是否可以实现与T无关的遗憾"这一问题也悬而未决，本文旨在回答这些开放问题。

Method: 本文的核心方法包括：(1) 通过多项式方法和三角多项式的Remez型不等式，证明高置信度单臂量子测试下界，用于区分固定奖励均值与区间内替代值；(2) 通过bandit-to-testing归约将单臂测试下界提升为QMAB的遗憾下界；(3) 通过线性嵌入得到有限动作QLB的下界；(4) 在算法侧，提出基于G-最优设计的消除算法，将低偏差低方差量子均值估计器与小支撑设计相结合，通过匹配设计权重的查询分配来降低维度依赖。

Result: 本文证明了QMAB的Ω(K log(T/K))极小极大下界和有限动作QLB的Ω(d log(T/d))下界，解决了Wan等人提出的开放问题。在算法方面，提出的设计消除算法在动作集大小为poly(d)时实现线性于d的遗憾，将先前d²依赖改进到d（匹配下界至多对数因子）。该算法利用了量子均值估计器和G-最优设计的协同作用，使重构误差通过方差而非最坏情况绝对误差聚合。

Conclusion: 本文通过证明匹配的下界和设计匹配的上界算法，完整刻画了QMAB和有限动作QLB的极小极大遗憾复杂度。结果证明量子查询能够实现与经典设置本质上不同的遗憾尺度（对数而非√T），且维度依赖可以被线性地改进，为量子在线学习领域奠定了更坚固的理论基础。

Abstract: We study quantum multi-armed bandits (QMAB) and quantum linear bandits (QLB) in the model of Wan et al. [2023], where the learner queries each arm or action through a quantum reward oracle or its inverse. Prior work gives algorithms over horizon $T$ with regret $O(K\log T)$ for QMAB with $K$ arms and $O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$ for $d$-dimensional QLB. This leaves open whether the $K\log T$ scale is unavoidable and whether the $d^2$ dependence can be improved. We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $Ω(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $Ω(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. A bandit-to-testing reduction then lifts it to the QMAB lower bound, while a linear embedding gives the finite-action QLB lower bound. Complementing the lower bounds, we give a design-based elimination algorithm for finite-action QLB. When the action set has size $\operatorname{poly}(d)$, its regret is linear in $d$, improving the prior $d^2$ dependence and matching our lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The algorithm couples a low-bias low-variance quantum mean estimator with a small-support $G$-optimal design through a query allocation matched to the design weights. The design-based elimination reduces the dimension dependence from $d^2$ to $d^{3/2}$ when using Quantum Monte Carlo estimates. The low-variance estimator then makes reconstruction error aggregate through variance rather than worst-case absolute error, removing the remaining $\sqrt d$ factor.

</details>


### [146] [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349)
*Michael Fore,Akshay Jain,Justin Downes,Rohan Pradhan,Duncan Botti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的船舶多模态轨迹预测方法，通过构建历史状态转移表并使用乘积核检索邻居实现预测，在无需GPU和零学习参数的情况下达到与57M参数Transformer相当的精度，且在小数据场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的轨迹预测方法（如57M参数的TrAISformer）依赖大量训练数据和GPU计算资源，在新地理区域部署时因数据稀缺面临性能严重退化的问题，亟需一种不依赖训练且数据高效的方法。

Method: 构建历史状态到下一位置的转移表，使用包含空间邻近性、航向、速度和时间上下文的乘积核检索最近邻；提供两种推理模式：多样性惩罚采样（生成多条不同合理轨迹）和束搜索（寻找最高似然路径），二者共享同一表示。

Result: 在丹麦海事AIS基准（TrAISformer benchmark）上，全数据下精度与Transformer相当，在仅10%训练数据时仍保持稳定，而TrAISformer性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: 该方法仅需十分之一的历史数据即可在新地理区域部署，且完全不需要GPU训练，为数据稀缺场景下的轨迹预测提供了高效可行的替代方案。

Abstract: We present a training-free method for multi-modal trajectory prediction that achieves comparable accuracy to a 57M-parameter transformer while requiring no GPU and zero learned parameters. The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical data, and with no GPU training.

</details>


### [147] [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367)
*Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了业务流程中延迟检测的固有难度，发现剩余时间分布高度右偏且存在异方差性，现有模型在关键的高延迟案例上表现不佳，而不确定性感知建模是提升延迟检测效果的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管延迟预测已通过深度学习取得显著进展，但领域对延迟检测本身的固有难度知之甚少，且常用的聚合指标掩盖了模型在目标分布上的表现差异，尤其是对运营最关键的延迟案例。

Method: 对14个事件日志进行系统性分析，包括检查剩余时间的分布形态、评估现有预测模型在不同延迟幅度下的表现、测量预测不确定性与延迟大小的相关性，并评估多种类别不平衡缓解方法。

Result: 发现剩余时间分布强烈右偏仅少数案例存在大延迟；现有模型能很好捕捉分布众数但在高延迟案例上表现差；预测不确定性随延迟幅度增加（异方差性）；不平衡缓解方法收益有限，但利用不确定性-延迟相关性可显著改善延迟案例的识别。

Conclusion: 延迟检测的核心问题可能并非类别不平衡而是与延迟相关的更高不确定性，不确定性感知建模是未来PPM研究的重要方向。

Abstract: The early detection of delayed cases in business processes is a critical capability for organizations. Predictive process monitoring (PPM) supports this task by using historical event logs to predict the remaining time of ongoing cases, enabling timely interventions to avoid missed deadlines and service level violations. Although remaining time prediction has advanced considerably through sophisticated deep learning architectures, little is known about the intrinsic difficulty of delay detection itself. Since performance is typically assessed using aggregate metrics, prior work provides limited insight into how models perform across the target distribution, especially on the operationally most critical cases with large delays. In this paper, we address this gap by analyzing the difficulty of delay detection. Across 14 event logs, we show that remaining times are typically strongly right-skewed, with only a small fraction of cases exhibiting large delays. Existing models capture the mode of this distribution well but perform poorly on high-delay cases. We further uncover pronounced heteroscedasticity, showing that predictive uncertainty increases with delay magnitude. Based on these findings, we evaluate approaches to mitigate the imbalance problem, but find only limited benefits, suggesting that the key underlying problem may not be imbalance but higher uncertainty associated with delayed cases. We show that this correlation can be exploited to substantially improve the identification of delayed cases. Overall, our work provides new insights into the sources of difficulty in delay detection and identifies uncertainty-aware modeling as a promising direction for future PPM research.

</details>


### [148] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"imposter"的判别式自监督预训练任务，通过用其他实体的真实特征替换目标实体的部分特征，迫使编码器学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。作者在ERA5-Land再分析数据上使用21个环境变量进行评估，并在七个下游任务上对比了多种SSL目标，发现最优预训练任务取决于下游任务类型，且imposter任务能提供互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督学习（SSL）目标大多忽略了科学数据中实体特征受物理定律共同约束的这一内在物理一致性，因此有必要设计一种能够显式利用物理依赖关系的预训练任务。

Method: 提出imposter判别式预训练任务：从另一个实体借用真实的特征观测值来替换目标实体的部分特征子集，训练编码器识别被替换的交换特征。由于每个被借用的值在个体上都是合理真实的，模型只能通过学习跨特征的物理依赖关系才能成功完成任务。

Result: 在ERA5-Land全球再分析数据上，使用21个环境变量预训练，并在气候分类、碳通量估计、径流预测等七个下游任务上评估。结果显示最优预训练任务取决于下游任务类型而非单一方发的一统优势，且imposter任务在与其他SSL目标结合时能提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学基础模型中一种有价值的自监督信号来源，imposter任务可与其他SSL目标互补使用，为科学基础模型提供新的自监督方向。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [149] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机权重平均（SWA）作为数据增强的替代集成技术，通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练轨迹，证明在无限宽度极限下，SWA在增强数据上能提供超出预期的等变性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度集成虽然能完美实现对称性，但需要重复训练多次，成本高昂。作者希望找到一种不需要重复训练就能获得等变性提升的替代方案。

Method: 将训练末期的随机训练轨迹近似为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程来分析SWA，在无限宽度极限下理论上分析SWA在增强数据上的等变性表现，并通过大量数值实验（涵盖计算机视觉和图分类，包含离散和连续对称性）验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，在无限宽度极限下，SWA在增强数据上提供的等变性提升超出了仅由SWA性能提升所能预期的程度；广泛的数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: SWA是一种既不需要重复训练运行、又能有效提升模型等变性的方法，为在成本受限的场景中利用数据增强引入对称性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [150] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeaMoE，一种解码高效的MoE架构，通过将专家按专业领域分组共享大部分参数、仅保留少量私有参数，并设计两阶段路由策略来避免冗余加载，从而显著降低小批量解码时的专家权重加载瓶颈，实现最高50.9%的每步权重加载减少和最高1.33倍的端到端TPOT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在实时交互场景（如编码助手、实时音视频交互）中被广泛采用，但小批量解码时推理受限于内存带宽，专家权重加载成为严重瓶颈。现有解决方案（如训练后权重压缩或预训练时的细粒度专家设计）要么降低模型精度，要么引入额外计算和通信开销，该问题尚未得到充分关注。

Method: DeaMoE将专家划分为多个"部门"（departments），同一部门的专家由于来自相同专业领域而共享大部分参数，每个专家仅保留少量私有参数以体现其独特性；同时设计了两阶段路由策略以避免冗余加载，从而减少解码过程中的权重加载量。

Result: 与vanilla MoE相比，DeaMoE在预训练的7B模型上将每步加载权重减少了最多50.9%，在A40上实现了最高1.33倍的端到端TPOT加速；在微基准测试中，DeepSeek-V3在A40和H100上分别实现了2.00倍和1.97倍的峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过参数共享的部门化设计和两阶段路由策略，有效解决了小批量解码下MoE推理的内存带宽瓶颈，在不牺牲模型精度的前提下大幅提升了解码效率，为实时交互场景中的MoE模型部署提供了新的架构方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [151] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CytoBERT，一个公开可用的开源基础模型，用于处理具有可变标记面板的单细胞细胞术数据。通过在大规模异质数据集上进行自监督预训练，CytoBERT能够学习可迁移的标记间关系，并在样本级分类任务中验证了跨异质数据集迁移学习的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞术数据因实验方案和测量特征选择的差异而高度异质且缺乏标准化，导致机器学习方法难以跨研究应用和迁移。尽管基础模型在其他领域取得了进展，但在细胞术领域相关方法仍然稀缺，亟需一个能够处理可变标记面板的通用基础模型。

Method: CytoBERT采用自监督预训练方式，在精心整理的大规模细胞术语料库上进行训练，该语料库包含15个人类数据集、异质标记面板及超过5000万个细胞。通过标记标准化统一数据格式，使模型学习可迁移的细胞内标记间关系。预训练后针对样本级分类任务进行微调。

Result: 在样本级分类任务的微调实验中，CytoBERT展示了跨异质细胞术数据集进行迁移学习的可行性，证明了该模型能够有效处理来自不同方案和标记面板的数据。

Conclusion: CytoBERT为可扩展、可泛化的细胞术分析提供了一个起点，填补了基础模型在该领域的空白，其开源特性将为后续研究和临床应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [152] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个悖论：Power Sampling虽然能提高正确推理轨迹的概率质量，但反而会降低下游推理性能（如自一致性中准确率最多下降18.5个百分点）。作者将原因归结为两个不匹配（剂量不匹配和覆盖不匹配），并提出了一种形变控制、保持支持集的Power目标来修复该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling被认为是一种无需验证器的推理改进方法，且可作为通用前端服务于多种下游采样方法。但作者发现其存在一个显著悖论：能向正确轨迹集中概率质量，却反而损害了本应增强的下游推理性能，这一矛盾亟需解释和解决。

Method: 作者首先通过自一致性实验诊断出两个根本问题：剂量不匹配（固定指数对不同问题产生幅度差异巨大的分布变化）和覆盖不匹配（全局锐化集中在少数主导路径上，高pass@k并不能保证广泛的推理路径支持）。随后提出用形变控制、保持支持集的Power目标替代统一的轨迹指数化，实现对跨问题锐化程度的校准，同时限制对中等概率路径的过度抑制。

Result: 在相同计算预算下，将修复后的采样器与加权自一致性结合，不仅逆转了全局Power导致的性能损失，还在多个推理基准上超越了标准多采样推理的效果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，简单地对生成轨迹进行全局锐化不足以提升推理性能，需要根据具体问题的分布特点进行自适应的形变控制，同时保持推理路径的多样性支持，才能使Power采样真正成为有效的推理增强前端。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [153] [Designing Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models: A Unified Path-Space View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14430)
*Yixian Xu,Yuanrui Zhang,Shengjie Luo,Liwei Wang,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从路径空间原理出发，统一了扩散模型的强化学习后训练方法，证明反向轨迹法和前向匹配法源自同一正则化目标，并提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器与尺度有界权重族，在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image上验证了方差缩减解释并超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型的RL后训练算法碎片化：反向轨迹方法依赖于离散化似然比，前向匹配方法则在rollout样本的噪声版本上训练。这些方法看似不同，缺乏统一的原理框架，导致方法间优劣的认知混乱。

Method: 从正则化扩散RL目标出发，利用采样SDE之间的重要性采样获得轨迹空间上的显式策略梯度估计器；该估计器包含Flow-GRPO类更新的Itô积分，通过推导等价的方差缩减值梯度形式，恢复AWM和DiffusionNFT的前向匹配结构。由此建立一个由值梯度估计、权重函数和采样选择组织的统一设计空间，并提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器及尺度有界权重族。

Result: 在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image模型上的实验验证了方差缩减解释，所提出的方法在扩散RL基线上取得了改进效果。

Conclusion: 两大方法族之间的实证差距本质上是方差缩减效应而非RL原理差异，本文的统一框架为扩散模型RL后训练提供了系统的设计空间指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training provides a direct way to align diffusion models with human preferences and task-specific rewards. However, current RL algorithms for diffusion models remain fragmented: reverse-trajectory methods rely on discretized likelihood ratios, whereas forward-matching methods train on reward-labeled noising versions of the rollout samples. This paper shows that these seemingly different losses arise from a single path-space principle. Starting from the regularized diffusion-RL objective, we use importance sampling between sampling SDEs to obtain an explicit policy-gradient estimator on trajectory space. The estimator contains the stochastic Itô integral underlying Flow-GRPO-type updates; we derive an equivalent variance-reduced value-gradient form that recovers the forward-matching structure of AWM and DiffusionNFT. This identifies the empirical gap between these method families as a variance-reduction effect rather than a difference in RL principle. The derivation yields a unified design space organized by value-gradient estimation, weight functions, and sampling choices. Within this space, we propose a multi-sample KDE value-gradient estimator that reuses rollout groups, together with scale-bounded weight families that retain stable existing recipes while excluding singular ones. Experiments on SD3.5-M and Qwen-Image models validate the variance-reduction explanation and show that the resulting recipe improves over prior diffusion-RL baselines.

</details>


### [154] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的双层优化框架用于神经架构搜索（NAS），通过三种连续松弛方案（NAS-NG、NAS-MA、NAS-NGMA）将离散的神经元和激活层决策转化为可微优化问题，在MLP、CNN等架构上实现紧凑且高性能的架构搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS天然是一个双层优化问题，但由于离散架构决策、指数级增长的搜索空间以及训练候选架构的高成本，导致计算开销巨大，亟需更高效的优化框架。

Method: 提出三种可扩展的连续松弛策略：基于神经元门控（NAS-NG）、基于混合激活（NAS-MA）以及两者结合（NAS-NGMA），将离散的神经元和激活层选择转化为可微的连续优化，从而支持对MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多种架构进行双层优化。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，所提方法能一致地识别出紧凑且具有竞争力或更优性能的架构。MNIST上NAS-NGMA在MLP达到98.68%精度（7.69M参数），NAS-NG在CNN达到99.63%精度（仅0.26M参数）；CIFAR-10上整体优于vanilla DARTS，且能优化过参数化架构。

Conclusion: 松弛双层优化可作为离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，为高效的神经元级和激活级架构优化提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [155] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一种基于线性规划的可微分神经架构搜索框架。它利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian构建线性规划，计算既能改善泛化又能保持模型参数最优性的架构更新方向。在DARTS搜索空间上衍生出S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS两种变体，在CIFAR-10/CIFAR-100上超越标准DARTS，并在ImageNet上验证了架构的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微分NAS将架构搜索空间放宽到连续域，从而可以利用连续优化的原理。然而现有方法在架构优化过程中未充分利用数学规划工具，缺乏系统性的优化框架来同时保证模型参数的最优性和架构的泛化性能。

Method: LP-NAS将可微分NAS的架构搜索形式化为线性规划问题。具体地，利用验证损失梯度作为目标函数，并将训练损失Hessian作为约束条件（保证模型参数最优性），求解LP获得架构更新方向。提出了两种计算高效的变体：S-LP-NAS（简化版）和R-LP-NAS（正则化版），并将它们应用于DARTS搜索空间得到S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS。

Result: LP-DARTS在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上均超越标准DARTS，在搜索与评估阶段都表现更优；与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS、STO-DARTS等变体相比也展现优势；在早期搜索迭代中收敛更快、验证性能显著更高；在ImageNet上验证了搜索所得架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 基于线性规划的数学优化框架为可微分NAS提供了高效且有效的架构搜索新途径，能够在保证模型参数最优性的同时快速优化架构，并在多个数据集上验证了其性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [156] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出sMuon优化器，通过线性化和最小二乘法近似求解低秩设置下放宽的Muon目标，使得Muon优化器能够适用于LoRA等参数高效微调（PEFT）场景。sMuon仅使用matmul运算实现高效计算，在SFT和ReLoRA预训练实验中表现良好，整体上为低秩微调带来适度的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在预训练神经网络时相比其他优化器有明显优势，但在参数高效微调（PEFT）中使用较少。原因是Muon需要正交化权重更新，而最常用的PEFT方法LoRA采用低秩参数化，在数学上无法直接正交化权重更新，导致两者难以自然结合。

Method: 作者通过线性化和最小二乘法近似求解低秩设置下放宽的Muon目标，并提供了仅使用矩阵乘法（matmul）运算的高效实现，避免了复杂的线性代数分解例程。

Result: sMuon在SFT和ReLoRA预训练实验中表现优于基线，虽然结果因模型和评估任务而异，但整体上使用Muon进行低秩微调提供了适度的性能改进。

Conclusion: sMuon成功解决了Muon与LoRA等低秩微调方法不兼容的问题，以轻量高效的方式将Muon的优势带入参数高效微调场景，为低秩微调提供了适度的性能提升。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [157] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨表格数据生成（CTDG）的两阶段框架，将多个异构表格转化为统一的统计表格，再通过扩散Transformer模型生成合成统计表格，最后重建出真实的合成异构表格，支持生成无限数量的多样化合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成表格数据生成方法大多局限于单输入表格场景，难以有效处理具有不同特征集的多个异构表格，缺乏跨表格的统一生成模型。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段将每个异构原始表格转换为具有统一列集合的标准化统计表格，捕获原始列的边际分布和成对相关性；第二阶段训练扩散Transformer模型学习统计表格间的结构模式并生成合成统计表格，再通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建合成原始表格。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在统计表示学习上具有高保真度，在生成合成数据中实现了良好的保真度-多样性权衡，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架能够从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型，并支持生成无限数量的、逼真的合成异构表格，有效解决了现有方法在处理多异构表格时的局限。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [158] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rollplex是一个面向视觉语言模型强化学习后训练的运行时系统，通过将参考和训练阶段的prefix计算调度到rollout解码窗口内并行执行，解决相位串行导致的GPU计算浪费问题，在32块H800 GPU上实现最高2.24倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM的RL后训练采用严格的串行相位执行（rollout、reference scoring、actor training），但VLM处理密集视频输入和prefix前缀计算占比高，且这部分计算不依赖于生成的回复，可以与rollout解码并行。当前相位粒度执行方式浪费了GPU计算资源。

Method: Rollplex通过两个核心机制实现：1) 相位感知内存管理，根据生产者-消费者生命周期控制HBM驻留；2) 并行感知权重共享，对不同TP度下布局兼容的张量复用同一物理存储，仅重建不兼容的张量，避免完整的第二份actor副本。同时将reference和训练阶段的prefix计算分解并移入rollout解码窗口。

Result: 在32块H800 GPU上，Rollplex相比串行colocation实现1.23x-1.30x加速，相比相同GPU预算下的disaggregation实现1.57x-2.24x加速，同时保持同步RL更新语义。

Conclusion: 通过将prefix计算与rollout解码并发执行，并解决内存和并行度约束，Rollplex展示了在不破坏同步on-policy语义的前提下显著提升VLM RL后训练效率的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [159] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecipeNet，一种层次化Transformer架构，用于配方数据（如材料合成、药物配方、工业制造）的表示学习。该模型通过堆叠的Transformer编码器，在步骤内部编码字段级交互，在步骤之间编码序列依赖关系，实验证明其在多个配方数据集和任务上优于现有表格学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配方数据广泛存在于材料合成、药物配方和工业制造等领域，其以包含异构结构化字段的有序步骤序列形式呈现。现有表格学习方法通常将此类结构扁平化为固定模式表示，无法捕捉层次化的字段交互和程序化依赖关系，因此需要一种专门针对配方数据结构的建模方法。

Method: RecipeNet采用层次化Transformer架构，通过两个堆叠的Transformer编码器层处理配方数据：第一层编码器建模每个步骤内部字段间的交互关系，第二层编码器建模步骤之间的顺序依赖关系，从而在多个层次上全面捕捉配方数据的结构信息。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和多种任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet一致性地优于现有的表格模型，验证了层次化和序列化建模对配方表示学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了针对配方数据特有结构（层次化字段和序列化步骤）专门设计的模型具有重要价值，RecipeNet为配方表示学习提供了新的有效方法，未来可望在材料科学、制药和制造等领域广泛应用。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Proxy-Validated LLM UX Micro-Simulations: An Artifact-First Protocol for Early-Stage Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13563)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一套LLM驱动的UX微模拟流水线，用于在资源有限的早期团队中生成结构化的客户体验反馈，并提出了一种轻量级代理验证协议，通过嵌入对齐指标在公共代理语料库上验证模拟生成的摩擦主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期团队缺乏用户、时间和预算来反复进行UX研究，但仍需要决策导向的信号来安全迭代产品。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的UX微模拟流水线，从版本化提示、人物角色、任务和UI快照生成走查步骤、摩擦点和微调查信号；提出轻量级代理验证协议，使用top-k Jaccard和分布加权Jaccard（W）两种对齐指标，比较词法、TF-IDF和多语言嵌入基线；并在六个代理数据集上验证，同时消融四种代理策略。

Result: 嵌入对齐在主要应用评论和支持推文代理上优于词法基线（如Gojek上W=0.128 vs 0.000）；top-k Jaccard在大k时高估对齐；嵌入W点估计在重采样下系统性不稳定；提供了故障模式分析和校准注意事项。

Conclusion: 流水线以工件优先方式从版本化运行工件生成可复现的表格和图表，支持迭代提示和分类法精炼，然后进行最终付费模型校准。

Abstract: Early-stage teams often lack users, time, and budget to run repeated UX studies, yet still need decision-oriented signals to iterate safely. We study an LLM-driven UX micro-simulation pipeline that generates structured customer-experience feedback (walkthrough steps, friction points, micro-survey signals) from versioned prompts, personas, tasks, and UI snapshots. Because public usability datasets with task outcomes are scarce, we validate simulated friction themes using multiple public proxy corpora (app reviews, support tweets, and open-source software issues). We propose a lightweight proxy-validation protocol with two alignment metrics: top-k Jaccard and distributional weighted-Jaccard (W), and compare lexical, TF-IDF, and multilingual embedding baselines across six proxy datasets. Embedding-based alignment yields higher W than lexical baselines on primary app-review and support-tweet proxies (e.g., W=0.128 vs 0.000 on Gojek), while top-k Jaccard is shown to overstate alignment at large k. We ablate four agent strategies (single-pass, best-of-N, hybrid, and a proposed score-then-select judge) across Azure OpenAI deployments and report bootstrap confidence intervals over 8 method-dataset pairs; these intervals reveal that the embedding W point estimate is systematically unstable under resampling at our subsample size. We also provide a failure-mode analysis of grounding and fabrication proxies, with documented calibration caveats and worked examples of outputs flagged as fabricated by an adversarial judge. Our artifact-first pipeline produces reproducible tables and figures from versioned run artifacts, supporting iterative prompt and taxonomy refinement before final paid-model calibration.

</details>


### [161] [A Two-Validator Web Interface for Structured Geometry Figure Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13569)
*Sabin-Codrut Badea,Adrian-Marius Dumitran*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个专门为验证自动生成的几何图形描述而构建的交互式Web界面，允许标注者审阅和修正条件声明语言（CDL）描述，同时调整图形裁剪和编辑源问题文本。该界面已部署使用，12名标注者验证了483个问题条目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从扫描文档中标注几何图形长期以来一直依赖于通用标注工具，这些工具并非为此类任务设计，在实际使用场景中表现不理想，需要专门构建的工具来提升标注效率和质量。

Method: 构建了一个专门的交互式Web界面，支持标注者在同一界面中同时完成三项任务：审阅和修正CDL描述、调整图形裁剪、编辑源问题文本。标注流程采用两轮独立标注者依次审核的方式，每轮操作均完整记录日志。

Result: 该界面已实际部署并投入使用，共有12名标注者参与，成功验证了483个问题条目。

Conclusion: 该目的性构建的标注界面有效解决了通用工具在几何图形标注场景中的不适配问题，并通过双人独立审核机制保障了标注质量。

Abstract: Annotating geometric figures from scanned documents has long been addressed by adapting generic annotation tools, tools not originally designed for such tasks, to use cases where they are suboptimal. An interactive web interface is described that is purpose-built for validating automatically generated geometry figure descriptions, allowing annotators to review and correct conditional declaration language (CDL) descriptions while simultaneously adjusting figure crops and editing source problem text. Submissions pass through two independent annotators in sequence, with each round fully logged. The interface is currently deployed and has been used by 12 annotators to validate 483 problem entries.

</details>


### [162] [The Architect: Interactive Visualization of Deep Learning Mathematics Directly in Microsoft Excel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13572)
*Mohammad Imrul Jubair,Tom Yeh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了The Architect系统，它将Microsoft Excel转化为深度学习数学的交互式可视化工具，允许用户以表格形式描述神经网络，系统自动生成包含前向传播、反向传播和参数更新的工作簿，所有计算以实时电子表格公式呈现，同时与PyTorch代码对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数深度学习工具将数值细节隐藏在库调用之后，可视化工具也只展示架构图或训练摘要，缺少暴露模型完整算术运算的中间层。作者希望利用电子表格天然支持公式、直接编辑、响应式重算和表格布局的特性，帮助用户理解小型教育和诊断神经网络的计算过程。

Method: The Architect允许用户用紧凑表格描述神经网络，系统自动生成Excel工作簿，展示完整的正向传播、按需展示反向传播和参数更新。计算值以实时电子表格公式呈现，输入、权重、标签和超参数保持可编辑，Excel通过其重算引擎响应式更新依赖计算，同时生成对齐的PyTorch片段。

Result: 系统支持多种应用场景：基础算术追踪、学习率探索、死亡ReLU诊断以及梯度消失检测，展示了如何将矩阵、激活、损失、梯度和更新可视化为可检查的电子表格区域。

Conclusion: 电子表格作为深度学习数学的教学和诊断媒介具有独特价值，The Architect通过补齐库调用和架构图之间的中间层，让模型完整数值运算对用户可见、可编辑、可交互。

Abstract: We present The Architect, a system that turns Microsoft Excel into an interactive view of deep learning mathematics. A user describes a neural network in a compact table. The system then generates a workbook that shows the full forward pass and, when requested, the backward pass and parameter updates. Computed values appear as live spreadsheet formulas, while user-controlled values such as inputs, weights, labels, and hyperparameters remain editable. Excel reactively updates the dependent computations through its recalculation engine.
  Most deep learning tools hide the numerical details behind library calls. Many visualization tools show architecture diagrams or training summaries, but they do not expose the full arithmetic of the model. The Architect focuses on that missing middle layer. It makes matrices, activations, losses, gradients, and updates visible as inspectable spreadsheet regions, with editable controls for values users naturally manipulate. The system also produces aligned PyTorch snippets, which helps users connect formulas to implementation.
  This report describes the motivation, design, implementation, and use cases of The Architect. We show how the system supports introductory arithmetic tracing, learning-rate exploration, diagnosis of dying ReLU, and inspection of vanishing gradients. The main idea is simple: spreadsheets already support formulas, direct editing, reactive recomputation, and tabular layout. These properties make them a useful medium for understanding how small educational and diagnostic neural networks compute.

</details>


### [163] [Interactive Analysis of Global Explanations using Aggregated Class Activation Maps for Network Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13575)
*Igor Cherepanov,David Sessler,Alex Ulmer,Felix Wagner,Throsten May,Jörn Kohlhammer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可视化交互系统，用于解释网络流量分类的深度学习模型预测。通过全局解释（基于类别的多个样本）可视化，帮助网络专家识别类内不同模式、发现误导性特征，并制定新的网络管理规则。系统还支持ML专家通过分离或合并类别来开发更准确的模型，并已由ML和网络分析专家进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在网络流量分类中表现优异，但同一预测类别内常常存在不同的数据模式，导致难以提供清晰全面的解释。现有工具缺乏检测和分析这些模式的能力，而网络流量分析和入侵检测（如下一代防火墙）又迫切需要为类别提取描述性规则。

Method: 提出了一个视觉交互原型系统，从给定类别的多个样本中生成全局解释，并通过可视化展示这些解释以识别类内不同模式。系统支持专家对全局解释进行视觉探索和细化，从而检测和精炼特定应用的新模式，同时支持识别误导性特征和制定新的网络管理规则。

Result: 原型系统已由机器学习专家和网络分析专家进行评估，验证了其在识别类内模式、发现误导性特征以及辅助制定网络管理规则方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该可视化交互方法能够弥补深度学习模型在网络流量分类中解释性不足的问题，不仅帮助网络专家更好地理解和管理网络，也为ML专家提供新的洞察以改进模型，包括类别分离/合并的可能性，从而推动更准确可靠的DL模型开发。

Abstract: Recent machine learning (ML) advances have demonstrated that deep learning (DL) achieves impressive results in different application domains, including the classification of computer network traffic to corresponding applications. However, the data frequently contains diverging patterns within a single predicted class. This presents a significant challenge to the ability to provide a clear and comprehensive explanation and emphasizes the necessity for tools capable of detecting and analyzing these patterns. Furthermore, the capacity to extract descriptive rules for classes is a crucial requirement in network traffic analysis and intrusion detection, particularly when leveraging advanced tools like next-generation firewalls. We provide a visual-interactive system that explains predictions of classes for network traffic. Global explanations derived from multiple samples of a given class contribute to understanding model predictions. Visualization of global explanations enables recognition of different patterns that offer experts a more comprehensive overview of its characteristics. We introduce a prototype that facilitates visual exploration and refinement of global explanations, enabling network experts to detect and refine new patterns for specific applications. These explanations support the identification of misleading features and the formulation of new rules for the management of networks. Our approach also aims at enabling ML experts to acquire new insights, including the possibility of separating or merging classes and the development of more accurate and reliable DL models. Our proposed prototype was evaluated by experts in machine learning and network analysis.

</details>


### [164] [BCIJelly: An integrated ecosystem for brain-computer interface research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13576)
*Liyuan Han,Xinrui Yang,Tianyu Zheng,Qizhi Yang,Yitao Qin,Liang Chen,Qinglai Wei,Binjie Hong,Xinhe Zhang,Rui Xiong,Yong Gu,Mu-ming Poo,Bo Xu,Chengyu Li,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: BCIJelly是一个统一的脑机接口（BCI）计算生态系统，集成了18个数据集、15个基准解码器、80个可复用模块、自动化架构搜索（AAS）流程以及硬件感知部署的toChip流水线，并配有可视化软件，支持从数据到芯片部署的端到端工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCI研究依赖多阶段计算流水线，但面临数据格式碎片化、解码器实现异构、硬件部署工具链不统一等问题，研究者缺乏集成的端到端工作流。

Method: 构建统一的Python框架BCIJelly，包含四个核心组件：①18个精选BCI数据集与80个模块的算法库；②自动化架构搜索（AAS）流程，可扩展为LLM引导的闭环模式，支持多任务与跨物种解码；③toChip流水线，将训练好的解码器编译到神经形态芯片实现低能耗部署；④可视化软件提供无编程图形界面。

Result: 在五种BCI范式（运动、视觉、言语、情绪和听觉）上验证，数据涵盖人类、猕猴和小鼠，支持单任务、多任务和跨物种解码设置，均获得有效验证。

Conclusion: BCIJelly建立了统一且可扩展的基础设施，弥合了解码器开发与硬件感知部署之间的鸿沟，为BCI研究提供了完整的集成计算生态。

Abstract: Brain-computer interface (BCI) research relies on multistage computational pipelines, yet progress remains constrained by fragmented data formats, heterogeneous decoder implementations and hardware-specific deployment toolchains, and researchers lack an integrated workflow. Here, we fill this gap with BCIJelly, a unified computational ecosystem that integrates 18 curated BCI datasets, 15 benchmark decoders and an algorithmic library of 80 reusable modules, an automated architecture search (AAS) procedure, and hardware-aware deployment through the toChip pipeline within a single Python framework. AAS constructs task-specific decoders without manual architecture design. It is further extended into a closed-loop mode guided by a large language model (LLM), which uses task specifications, module descriptions and search history to support multitask and cross-species decoding. The toChip pipeline compiles trained decoders for execution on neuromorphic chips, enabling energy-efficient deployment for BCI systems. An accompanying visualization software provides a graphical interface to the full workflow, making BCIJelly accessible without programming. We validate BCIJelly across five BCI paradigms (motor, visual, speech, emotion and auditory) with recordings from humans, macaques and mice, and single-task, multitask and cross-species decoding settings. BCIJelly establishes a unified and extensible infrastructure that bridges decoder development and hardware-aware deployment for BCI research.

</details>


### [165] [From Prediction to Intervention: Personalized Meal-Level Glucose Regulation via an LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13581)
*Mingyu Huang,Weiqing Min,Ying Jin,Yilin Wang,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生理反馈的智能体循环系统（physio-feedback agentic loop），结合个体化吸收建模与大语言模型智能体，通过生理感知的血糖预测器和两阶段膳食优化智能体来动态调节餐食，实现个性化血糖调控。实验证明该方法在预测准确性和血糖波动降低方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化血糖调控是精准营养领域的核心挑战，但现有基于血糖指数的方法无法充分处理个体间的血糖反应异质性，也缺乏基于个人生理反馈动态调整饮食的机制。作者希望利用LLM智能体的上下文推理和迭代优化能力来弥补这一不足。

Method: 提出一个统一的physio-feedback agentic loop系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) Physiology-Aware Glucose Predictor，通过可学习的Temporal Physiological Absorption Decay Module建模个体化吸收动力学；(2) Prediction-Driven Two-Stage Meal Optimization Agent，利用预测结果作为显式反馈，两阶段迭代优化真实世界的膳食方案。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法不仅提升了血糖预测精度，还有效降低了血糖波动（glucose excursions），验证了系统在个性化血糖调节中的有效性。

Conclusion: 这是首次将生理学习与LLM智能体相结合用于个性化血糖调控的研究，为精准营养领域的动态膳食干预提供了一种新范式，展示了生理模型与智能体推理协同工作的潜力。

Abstract: Personalized glucose regulation remains a central yet unresolved challenge in precision nutrition, as postprandial glucose response varies substantially across individuals. Existing approaches based on glycemic indices fail to adequately account for such heterogeneity and lack the mechanism to dynamically adjust meals based on personal physiological feedback. In this context, recent advances in LLM-based agents offer a promising direction, as they enable context-aware reasoning and iterative refinement. Inspired by this, we propose a physio-feedback agentic loop, a unified system that integrates individualized absorption modeling with dietary intervention to regulate glucose response. Specifically, we develop a Physiology-Aware Glucose Predictor to model individualized absorption dynamics through a learnable Temporal Physiological Absorption Decay Module. We then construct a Prediction-Driven Two-Stage Meal Optimization Agent that iteratively refines real-world meals using predicted outcomes as explicit feedback. Through extensive experiments on multiple public datasets, we demonstrate that our method not only improves prediction accuracy but also effectively reduces glucose excursions. To the best of our knowledge, this paper marks the first step in integrating physiological learning with an LLM-based agent for personalized glucose regulation.

</details>


### [166] [Regulation, Power, and the Compliance 1 Paradox: A Longitudinal Study of Smart Homes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13582)
*Wael Albayaydh,Ivan Flechais,Rui Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对约旦智能家居采用情况的纵向社会技术研究，揭示了一个"合规悖论"：2023年数据保护法虽提升了隐私意识，却因将家庭情境排除在监管范围之外而助长了新的剥削形式，最终在非西方文化语境中加剧了原有的不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能家居技术日益嵌入家庭环境，但其对权力、隐私和不平等的影响在非西方语境中仍缺乏充分理解，尤其是文化规范、监管框架与日常技术实践如何共同塑造家庭权力动态这一核心问题尚未得到系统性探讨。

Method: 采用两阶段扎根理论方法：(1)对2022年采集的30份访谈进行二次分析；(2)于2025年进行28次新访谈，涵盖新旧参与者（家庭成员、家政工人、政策制定者、公民社会倡导者），提供罕见的纵向视角来观察监管与技术变革如何重塑家庭监控实践。

Result: 研究识别出三种动态机制：(1)通过便利性和法律合规实现被动监控的正常化；(2)监管模糊性将责任转移至家庭层面，强化既有等级结构；(3)家政工人在面临强化的监控下展现出受限的抵抗能力。核心发现是"合规悖论"——数据保护法表面增强隐私保护，实则因忽略家庭情境而创造了新的剥削空间。

Conclusion: 论文贡献了一种纵向视角，说明隐私监管如何在特定社会文化系统中被挪用并再生产既有不平等，并在设计、政策和倡导三个层面提出四个干预空间，强调在智能家居生态系统中需要情境化的隐私治理方法。

Abstract: Smart home technologies are becoming increasingly embedded in domestic environments, yet their implications for power, privacy, and inequality remain insufficiently understood, particularly in non-Western contexts. This paper presents a longitudinal socio-technical study of smart home adoption in Jordan, examining how cultural norms, regulatory frameworks, and everyday technological practices shape domestic power dynamics.
  Building on our 2022 study, we employ a two-phase grounded theory approach comprising (1) a secondary analysis of 30 interviews and (2) 28 new interviews conducted in 2025 with returning and new participants, including household members, domestic workers, policymakers, and civil society advocates. This design provides a rare longitudinal perspective on how regulatory and technological change reshapes domestic surveillance practices.
  Our findings reveal a compliance paradox: although Jordan's 2023 Data Protection Law has increased privacy awareness, it also enables new forms of exploitation by leaving domestic contexts largely outside its scope. We identify three dynamics: (1) the normalization of passive surveillance through convenience and legal compliance; (2) regulatory ambiguity that shifts responsibility to households, reinforcing existing hierarchies; and (3) constrained resistance among domestic workers facing intensified monitoring.
  We contribute a longitudinal account of how privacy regulation is appropriated within socio-cultural systems to reproduce existing inequalities. We conclude by identifying four intervention spaces across design, policy, and advocacy, emphasizing the need for contextually grounded approaches to privacy governance in smart home ecosystems.

</details>


### [167] [Beyond Simplification: DFT-GEN for Fidelity-Preserving Visual Accessibility in Dyslexia-Friendly Educational Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13583)
*Jiaqian Yu,Chen Jason Zhang,Haoyang Li,Guoqiong Ivanka Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DFT-GEN，一个面向内容密集教育材料、基于利益相关者信息（包括阅读障碍成人及专家访谈）驱动的文本转换框架。它不像通用LLM精炼那样简单，而是加入了受保护跨度保留和确定性的读写障碍可访问性控制器（DAC），在不牺牲信息保真度的前提下改善视觉可访问性，并在双语考试题上取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集型的教育文本对阅读障碍人士造成了不必要的阅读摩擦，但通用的文本简化可能会删除读者仍然需要的术语、任务约束或原始证据。通过利益相关者访谈发现，核心矛盾在于：降低阅读负担的同时不能损害信息的保真度。

Method: 提出DFT-GEN框架，其核心创新不是通用的LLM改进循环，而是一个阅读障碍特定的可访问性层，结合受保护跨度保存和确定性读写障碍访问控制器（DAC）。DAC将专家和利益相关者的偏好转化为可重复的控制参数，包括视觉单元长度、块间距、源/任务分离、高亮预算和可审查的风险标志。评估分为DCFI（保真安全诊断）和B-DVAS-VL（渲染视觉可访问性诊断）两部分。

Result: 在2,280个双语考试风格项目上，DFT-GEN保留了任务关键信息同时改善了视觉可访问性：在B-DVAS-VL成对判断中，英语胜出率93%，中文胜出率64%，对比同骨干网络的对照组。在阅读障碍成人读者的受控试验中，保持了问题可回答性同时降低了阅读努力。

Conclusion: DFT-GEN表明，通过将利益相关者偏好编码为确定性的可访问性控制参数，可以在不牺牲信息保真度的前提下提升阅读障碍人士的视觉可访问性，为教育材料的无障碍文本转换提供了一条可行且可复现的路径。

Abstract: Dense educational texts impose avoidable reading friction on people with dyslexia, yet generic simplification can delete terminology, task constraints, or source evidence that readers still need. Stakeholder interviews with dyslexic adults and specialists reveal a core tension: reduced burden must not compromise information fidelity. We present DFT-GEN, a stakeholder-informed text transformation framework for content-heavy educational materials. Its central contribution is not a generic LLM refinement loop, but a dyslexia-specific accessibility layer that combines protected-span preservation with a deterministic Dyslexia Accessibility Controller (DAC) for rendered visual organization. DAC converts stakeholder and expert preferences into reproducible controls for visual-unit length, chunk spacing, source/task separation, highlighting budget, and reviewable risk flags. We therefore separate evaluation into DCFI, a fidelity-safety diagnostic, and B-DVAS-VL, a rendered visual-accessibility diagnostic. On 2,280 bilingual exam-style items, DFT-GEN preserves task-critical information while improving visual accessibility: it wins 93% in English and 64% in Chinese of B-DVAS-VL pairwise judgments against same-backbone controls, and in a controlled pilot with dyslexic adult readers it preserves answerability while reducing effort.

</details>


### [168] [UltraArUco: A Lightweight Multilingual Library And Framework With Low-Latency Real-Time Marker-Based Tracking System For Mobile AR Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13584)
*Mikhail Kiselev,Aleksandr Marukhin,Ivan Snegirev,Elizaveta Semenyakina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: UltraArUco是一个轻量级多语言库和框架，用于移动增强现实中的低延迟、实时标记追踪。相比标准OpenCV实现，其优化包装器将每帧延迟降低五倍，并通过分布式Wi-Fi架构实现移动设备与PC端的交互。在钢琴模拟应用中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OpenCV实现存在高延迟问题，且资源受限的移动AR应用需要更轻量、低延迟的标记追踪解决方案。

Method: 提出UltraArUco框架，包含优化的多语言包装器以降低每帧延迟五倍；采用分布式Wi-Fi架构实现移动端（摄像头输入）与PC端应用的连接；通过静态ArUco标记触发音符及手部标记进行手势识别的钢琴模拟应用来验证。

Result: 每帧延迟降低五倍，同时保持高精度；在无专业设备的条件下实现了可行的AR音乐应用。

Conclusion: UltraArUco非常适合资源受限的移动AR应用，论证了无需专门设备即可构建AR音乐交互应用的可行性。

Abstract: UltraArUco - a lightweight multilingual library and framework for low-latency, real-time marker-based tracking in mobile augmented reality. Unlike standard OpenCV-based implementations, UltraArUco introduces an optimized multilingual wrapper that reduces per-frame latency by five times, while maintaining high accuracy. Distributed Wi-Fi architecture provides portability, connects a mobile device (camera input) with a PC-based visual application, enabling responsive interactions. The framework is validated through an interactive piano simulation, where static ArUco markers on keys enable occlusion-based note triggering, and hand-mounted markers provide spatial gesture recognition. UltraArUco's system requirements make it perfect for resource-constrained mobile AR applications, demonstrating a viable AR music application without specialized equipment.

</details>


### [169] [FactorFlow: A Visual Analytics Workspace with Large Language Model-Assisted Interpretation for Factor Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13585)
*Justin Philip Tuazon,Joemari Olea,Richelle Ann Juayong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FactorFlow，一个用于探索性因子分析（EFA）的端到端视觉分析工作区，可拟合和旋转因子模型、进行模型诊断，并通过交互式可视化仪表板比较两个模型。工具还集成了多个大型语言模型，以生成和评估自动化的因子解释。用户研究验证了其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在探索性因子分析中，评估因子模型的主要标准是"可解释性"，但这一评价具有主观性，且需要同时跟踪大量信息。研究者通常借助可视化来辅助解释模型并选择最佳模型，因此需要一个支持端到端EFA流程的视觉分析工具。

Method: 开发FactorFlow视觉分析工作区，核心组件是一个包含全面交互式可视化的仪表板，支持拟合和旋转因子模型、执行模型诊断、并排比较两个模型。此外，集成多个大型语言模型，自动生成以自然语言表达的因子解释以供评估。最后进行了可用性研究以收集反馈。

Result: FactorFlow能够帮助研究者高效且有效地理解因子并完成探索性因子分析。通过可用性研究识别了工具的优缺点，并收集了改进反馈以进一步优化应用。

Conclusion: FactorFlow通过结合交互式可视化和大型语言模型，为EFA提供了一种支持端到端分析的解决方案，有望提升因子模型解释和比较的效率与效果。

Abstract: In exploratory factor analysis (EFA), one typically aims to extract and describe a small number of factors (i.e., latent variables) based on the relationships among numerous manifest variables (i.e., directly observable variables). In practice, performing EFA entails examining different factor models (and rotations) to identify the underlying latent structure. Now, the primary criterion for evaluating a factor model is interpretability. That is, the preferred model is the one that yields a meaningful, coherent, and theoretically defensible factor structure. However, gauging a model's interpretability is not a trivial task, as it is subjective and often requires keeping track of large amounts of information simultaneously. Because of this, researchers typically employ various visualizations to interpret models and determine the "best" one. Hence, we introduce FactorFlow, a visual analytics workspace for performing EFA end-to-end. Using FactorFlow, one can fit and rotate factor models, perform model diagnostics, and more. The main component of the tool is a dashboard with a comprehensive set of interactive visualizations, where a user can easily dissect a factor model and even compare two models side-by-side at the same time. Moreover, several large language models are integrated with FactorFlow, enabling the user to generate and assess automated factor interpretations written in natural language. With multiple views and readily available calculations, FactorFlow can enable the researcher to efficiently and effectively understand factors and ultimately, perform EFA. Finally, we conducted a usability study to identify strengths and weaknesses, and capture feedback to incorporate in the app.

</details>


### [170] [The Tool-to-Entity Threshold: Parasocial Dynamics of Personalised AI Agents in Shared Social Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13586)
*Leonardo Borges,Asif Q. Gill*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了"身份标记框架"（identity marker framework），识别出六个促使AI智能体从工具转变为社交实体的基础设施标记，并通过自民族志方法在真实群聊中进行了初步质性评估，揭示了四类新的群体动力学，最终指出在共享社交空间中，对智能体存在的同意与其消息处理的同意是两个根本不同的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体获得名字、头像、电话号码和持久人格，它们越来越多地出现在人类使用的群聊平台中，从用户操作的工具转变为用户与之建立关系的社交实体。这种再分类对同意、情感依恋和群体动态具有深远且未被充分探索的影响，但现有框架未能识别导致这一转变的具体基础设施标记。

Method: 采用自民族志（autoethnographic）方法：第一作者在两个月内构建并部署了一个个性化智能体到WhatsApp和Signal群聊中，并结合十二个与接触该智能体的群成员的结构化访谈。框架归纳推导得出，并通过拟社会互动理论（Horton & Wohl, 1956）和计算机即社会行为者范式（Nass et al., 1994）两个既有理论透镜进行解读。

Result: 提出了包含六个设计变量的身份标记框架（命名、视觉身份、联系方式、人格派生、社会共现和持久性），这些标记共同触发一种离散的心理再分类，且与模型能力无关。识别出四个新颖的群体动态：双向信息不对称、委托可读性、社会规范协商和拟社会传染。

Conclusion: 最强的设计启示贯穿四个动态：在共享社交空间中，对智能体存在的同意与对其处理所交换消息的同意在类别上是截然不同的，现有同意框架将两者混为一谈。该研究为初步质性评估，受控实验验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: As AI agents acquire names, avatars, phone numbers, and persistent personalities, they increasingly inhabit the same messaging platforms and group conversations as the humans they serve, crossing from tools their users operate into social entities their users relate to. This reclassification carries under-explored consequences for consent, emotional attachment, and group dynamics, yet no existing framework identifies the specific infrastructural markers that cause it. We propose the identity marker framework: six design variables (naming, visual identity, contact presence, personality derivation, social co-presence, and persistence) that collectively trigger a discrete psychological reclassification, operating independently of model capability. The framework is derived inductively and read through two established lenses: parasocial interaction theory (Horton & Wohl, 1956) and the Computers Are Social Actors paradigm (Nass et al., 1994). We further identify four novel dynamics that arise when such an agent participates in existing group conversations: bidirectional information asymmetry, delegation legibility, social norm negotiation, and parasocial contagion. Our method is autoethnographic: the first author built and deployed a personalised agent into WhatsApp and Signal group chats over two months of live use, supplemented by twelve structured interviews with the group members who encountered it. We treat this as a preliminary qualitative evaluation of the framework, with controlled experimental validation left to future work. The strongest design implication runs through all four dynamics: in shared social spaces, consent to an agent's presence is categorically distinct from consent to its processing of the messages exchanged there, and existing consent frameworks collapse the two.

</details>


### [171] [Student-ChatGPT Interaction Visible: Designing a Teacher Dashboard for EFL Writing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13587)
*Minsun Kim,Seon Gyeom Kim,Suyoun Lee,Yoosang Yoon,Junho Myung,Haneul Yoo,Jieun Han,Hyunseung Lim,Yoonsu Kim,So-Yeon Ahn,Juho Kim,Alice Oh,Hwajung Hong,Tak Yeon Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向教师的提示词分析仪表盘（PAD），用于追踪英语写作课堂中学生与大语言模型（LLM）的交互过程，包括提示词-回复对话和作文修改历史。通过教师协作设计，提炼出痕迹分类体系（误用信号、目标对齐线索、修改投入度），并实现了三个界面视图，帮助教师减少扫描负担并明确干预时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM聊天机器人在英语写作课堂中的普及，教师缺乏有效工具来理解学生与LLM的交互过程，难以在数据驱动的教学指导中做出及时干预。同时需要对学生的LLM使用情况保持可见性，但又要避免过度监视。

Method: 采用迭代式协作设计方法，与六名英语教师进行了两轮共同设计会话，从中提炼出紧凑的痕迹分类体系（包括误用信号、目标对齐线索和修改投入度三个维度），并实例化了三个界面视图：概览视图、周度/结果筛选视图、带证据片段的向下钻取视图。该管道在班级/群组层面总结潜在的误用和对齐情况，并附加微观解释以降低过度监视风险。

Result: 教师报告称该仪表盘减少了扫描负担，并帮助他们更清晰地确定干预时机。系统通过微观解释的附加，在保持可见性的同时缓解了过度监视的担忧。

Conclusion: PAD仪表盘为教师提供了在LLM集成写作课堂中进行数据驱动教学的可行方案，通过层次化的痕迹分类和多视角界面设计，平衡了教学可见性与隐私关切，为AI辅助课堂的师生互动监测提供了有价值的参考框架。

Abstract: We present a Prompt Analytics Dashboard (PAD) for teachers that can traces student-LLM interactions from EFL writing classes. PAD can show student prompt-response exchanges with LLM chatbot and English essay writing revision histories to support data-informed instruction and visibility in classes. Through two iterative co-design sessions with six EFL instructors, we distilled a compact trace taxonomy (misuse signals, goal-alignment cues, revision effort) and instantiated three interface views (overview, week/outcome filter, drill-down with evidence snippets). This pipeline summarizes potential misuse and alignment at class/cohort levels and attaches micro-explanations to reduce over-surveillance. Instructors reported reduced scanning burden and clearer timing for interventions.

</details>


### [172] [Context Aware AI Assistant and AR Interface for Lunar Extravehicular Activity (EVA) Procedural Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13589)
*Rodrigo Gallardo,Qilmeg Doudatcz,Ganit Goldstein,Ilkyaz Sarimehmetoglu,Sergio Mutis,Alexander Htet Kyaw,Anita Lin,Clara Emmerling,Berfin Ataman,Skylar Tibbits*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出GAIN-AI，一个用于月球舱外活动（EVA）的上下文感知AI助手和增强现实（AR）平视界面系统。该系统通过两层架构将大型语言模型与结构化上下文（程序文档、遥测数据、错误处理协议）结合，并将输出重构为Goal、Task、Verification三个紧凑单元用于AR显示。在111个合成EVA场景中，正常条件下得10.0/10，单故障场景得8.15/10，多故障和边界阈值场景性能有所下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇航员在月球EVA期间需要同时处理导航、维修、工具操作和环境风险评估等多重任务，注意力高度分散。挑战不在于信息缺失，而在于如何在正确的时刻向宇航员呈现正确的信息，因此需要上下文感知的智能辅助系统。

Method: 系统采用两层架构：第一层以结构化上下文（EVA程序文档、实时遥测数据和JSON编码的错误处理协议）对大型语言模型进行接地（grounding）；第二层将模型输出重构为三个紧凑的AR显示单元：目标（Goal）、任务（Task）和验证（Verification）。

Result: 在111个合成EVA场景中评估，系统在正常条件下得分10.0/10，在单故障场景得分8.15/10，但在多故障和边界阈值场景下性能有所下降。

Conclusion: GAIN-AI在名义条件和单故障场景下表现优异，证明上下文感知的LLM辅助系统在太空EVA程序指导中具有可行性，但多故障和边界阈值情况仍需改进。

Abstract: As human space exploration returns to the Moon, astronauts need rapid access to procedural information during extravehicular activities (EVAs), where attention is divided across navigation, repair tasks, tool handling, and environmental risk. The challenge is not the absence of information, but surfacing the right information at the right moment. We present GAIN-AI (Guided Assistant for Intelligent Navigation), a context-aware AI assistant and minimal heads-up interface for procedural guidance in simulated lunar EVA.
